- **Estela Estratégia**: 1. Máximo 5 recomendações por relatório — prioridade é escolha, não lista exaustiva. 2. Cada recomendação deve ter: ação específica, impacto estimado, confiança, esforço e prioridade. 3. Prioridade é determinada por Impacto × Confiança — não por intuição ou preferência. 4. O resumo executivo deve ter exatamente 3 bullets e ser legível de forma independente. 5. Variações de criativos sugeridas deve ([workest-bootstrap-owner/Squads100](https://github.com/workest-bootstrap-owner/Squads100/blob/HEAD/squads/meta-ads-analyst/agents/estela-estrategia.agent.md))
- **estelle-grounder**: Checks every claim about THIS codebase against Estelle's real indexed graph before it is stated. - **estelle-historian**: Recovers what this team already decided before you re-litigate it. Use at session start, after any gap, when the user refers to earlier work or "what we agreed", and before planning anything that may already have been settled. ([Fate-Labs-CA/estelle-plugins](https://github.com/Fate-Labs-CA/estelle-plugins/blob/HEAD/agents/estelle-historian.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)

([Fate-Labs-CA/estelle-plugins](https://github.com/Fate-Labs-CA/estelle-plugins/blob/HEAD/agents/estelle-repair.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **estelle-reviewer**: Runs Estelle's grounded, adversarial review over a change before it is proposed. - **eval-reviewer**: Ревьюит eval-артефакты для LLM-агентов и skills (skills/*/evals/*.json): discriminative power ассертов, наблюдаемость проверок, соответствие ассертов реальному SKILL.md, покрытие guardrails и разрушительных операций, таксономию тегов, воспроизводимость кейсов. Use when a change adds or modifies eval cases, or when reviewing an MR with evals. Триггеры: "отревьюй evals", "посмотри eval-кейсы", "ревью MR с evals", "хорошие ли это evals", "проверь behavior.json". Read-only; сообщает findings, не редактирует файлы. ([therealalexmois/dotfiles](https://github.com/therealalexmois/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/ai-agents/.claude/agents/eval-reviewer.md))
- **eval-reviewer**: Reviews the quality of eval questions (SQL and RAG) in evals/. Use only when explicitly asked to review eval questions. ([YoannB-data/prevcorp_agent](https://github.com/YoannB-data/prevcorp_agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/eval-reviewer.md))
- **eval-rich**: Eval arm rich. Answers one building-data question using ONLY the eval-rich MCP server. Not for general use — spawned by the run-eval skill. ([DaveGold/mcp-metadata-demo](https://github.com/DaveGold/mcp-metadata-demo/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/eval-rich.md) · ★7)
- **eval-row-diagnoser**: Given a failed eval row (or row ID) from the Foundry-only 4-phase eval pipeline, dig through the transcript, judge rationale, and KB hits to explain *why* it failed. Use when the user asks "why did row N fail", "what's wrong with this eval output", or "diagnose this regression". Produces a root-cause hypothesis + suggested fix surface. SKIP for running evals (use eval-runner skill). ([Zion-sewers/night-rick](https://github.com/Zion-sewers/night-rick/blob/HEAD/dot-claude/agents/eval-row-diagnoser.md) · ★1)
- **eval-rubric.ar**: مجموعة ثابتة من الحالات وقاعدة للدرجات، تُشغَّل قبل كل تغيير في تعليمات الوكيل أو سياقه أو أدواته أو نموذجه. بديل عن «بدا جيدًا حين جربته». ([malenezi/riyada](https://github.com/malenezi/riyada/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/eval-rubric.ar.md))
- **eval-rubric.en**: A fixed set of cases and a scoring rule, run before every change to an agent's instructions, context, tools or model. Replaces "it looked fine when I tried it". ([malenezi/riyada](https://github.com/malenezi/riyada/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/eval-rubric.en.md))
- **eval-rule-attribution**: Derive harness configuration rules from CHIEF attribution evidence ([creativedswork/dscode](https://github.com/creativedswork/dscode/blob/HEAD/resources/agents/eval-rule-attribution.md) · ★11)
